“After all, this gray power only suppresses the spirit. With my Limitless Fullness and Emptiness Method, even if my spirit is consumed, it can be quickly replenished.”

“The otherworldly illness, however, directly limits the upper bound of the spirit.”

Thinking this, Li Fan’s hands moved without pause.

One by one, the silver needles flew into Li Qing’s body, drawing out the continuously corrosive gray power.

As the culprit disappeared, Li Qing’s complexion gradually improved.

As for the extracted gray power, Li Fan temporarily sealed and preserved it using the Seven-Colored Divine Light.

He would study it later; perhaps there would be some surprises.

The treatment took the time of a full incense stick.

Once the last trace of gray power was eliminated, Li Fan finally exhaled in relief.

“The Record of Mending Heaven is indeed remarkable. A small test, and I’ve neutralized a great weapon that the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance is still experimenting with.”

The principle behind this treatment was actually simple.

It involved suppressing the spirit activity of his clone, Li Qing, then injecting the silver needles’ own spiritual power as bait to draw out the toxin.

Of course, to complete this treatment without harming the patient’s spirit required the healer to have extremely high spiritual proficiency.

If the patient were not his own clone, with whom Li Fan was intimately familiar, and if he did not have the Limitless Fullness and Emptiness Technique to endlessly replenish spiritual power, the treatment would not have been so easy.

After a while, Li Qing slowly awakened.

The first thing he said was: “The Sky-Patrolling Arrow’s targeting was not just bad luck. It should be that when I broke through the protective formation, I left some trace on my body.”

“That is why I was attacked by the Sky-Patrolling Arrow.”

Li Fan nodded slightly. “I thought of that too.”

“That new formation does have some tricks. Although it doesn’t look especially difficult to break, in retrospect, the flaw was deliberately exposed.”

“Like bait, when a formation master follows the exposed flaw and appears to break the formation, they have already quietly triggered a mark.”

“Using this mark, combined with the ever-present Sky-Patrolling Arrows within the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, they can carry out post-event assassinations.”

“It seems that Lu Xichan’s return was somewhat useful. A formation that appears defensive but is actually a trap requires skill to even conceive,” Li Fan said slowly.

Li Qing also sighed: “Fortunately, as a Dao Foundation cultivator of Heavenly Wonder, my flesh and blood are integrated with the Sword Dao. Even if blood is left behind, I need not fear being tracked. An ordinary cultivator, even if surviving the Sky-Patrolling Arrow, could be tracked for thousands of miles using the blood left on the arrow.”

“In any case, we should lie low for a while. The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance has prepared so many reserves; it seems they are planning a major move. All we can do is wait and see,” Li Fan instructed.

Li Qing nodded in agreement.

Then, a silver light containing the Xuantian Sect cultivation method flew from his mind.

Li Fan was extremely cautious and did not view it directly.

Instead, his clone read it, synchronizing cognition so that the contents of the method were recorded step by step in Li Fan’s own mind.

Each cultivation method of the Xuantian Sect far surpasses any existing technique in the Xuanhuang Realm today.

Chapter 929: Xuantian’s Lost Immortal Techniques

In the secret chamber of the Spiritwood Realm, simply reading through the texts once took Li Fan and his clone more than a month.

Among them, several techniques such as The Heaven and Earth Converging Manual and The Eternal Sole Dominance Art were almost completely consistent with the understanding Li Fan had gained in the illusionary realm.

In fact, the depictions in the illusionary realm were somewhat simplistic.

Take The Heaven and Earth Converging Manual for example. In the illusion, it was merely a dual-cultivation technique. When an insurmountable tribulation arrives that makes certain techniques incompatible, its effect of rapidly boosting cultivation would be neutralized.

However, in reality, this technique had another, even more profound and mystical use beyond dual cultivation.

It could lock onto a target and forcibly share the target’s fortune, cultivation speed, and even personal attributes such as comprehension.

This was the principle of “Heaven and Earth Sharing Longevity.”

For a true peerless prodigy, this technique would naturally seem trivial. But for someone like Li Fan, it was an exceptional mystical skill.

This was only the divine ability available at the initial stage of The Heaven and Earth Converging Manual.

As one cultivated it to a higher level, just as the name implies, the locked target could expand from a single person to the heavens and the earth.

“To use a mortal body to balance the power of heaven and earth...”

“Truly worthy of the immortal legacy.”

The upper limit of The Heaven and Earth Converging Manual was extraordinarily high, even hinting that it could still be used after ascending to the Immortal Realm.

Its only fatal flaw was that the locked target could sense the practitioner of the technique.

This was the meaning of “Conflict Sharing.”

Even this single drawback greatly reduced the technique’s significance in Li Fan’s mind.

As someone who preferred to plan and act strategically from the shadows, exposing himself to another person at all times was far from wise.

The only potential use he could think of now was to capture a peerless prodigy and confine them.

Then, he could forcibly equalize himself with the target, improving his own comprehension, aptitude, fortune, and so on.

Li Fan studied The Heaven and Earth Converging Manual, then briefly glanced through The Eternal Sole Dominance Art.

“Senior Sister Zhao actually trusted me with this Dao-attaining technique...”

After a moment of reflection, Li Fan suddenly understood.

Among all these techniques, The Eternal Sole Dominance Art was arguably the least valuable.

Because by the principle of “Unity of All,” it had already been dominated by Zhao Ruoxi.

There was no future path for it, and during cultivation, Senior Sister Zhao could very well perceive everything clearly...

Li Fan decisively sealed it and set it aside.

After roughly skimming through several other techniques, he then focused his attention on the two techniques he had encountered first:

The Secret Canon of Sitting in Oblivion, The Three Perils of Ghosts and Spirits in the Netherworld Record.

“The previous techniques came from one of the Twelve Dharma Kings, a giant man named Sikong Lin.”

“But these techniques...”

The faces of the twelve unique cultivators of the Xuantian Sect flashed through Li Fan’s mind one by one.

Quickly, Li Fan matched the style of the techniques to possible sources.

“Sun Xingwang.”

The aura emanating from these two techniques was simply too distinctive.

Take The Secret Canon of Sitting in Oblivion as an example. Its power was somewhat similar to the abilities of the former Xuantian King.

Both possessed a divine power akin to “words becoming law.”

The difference was that King Xuanyuan Hong’s words affected only himself, such as [Immense Strength] or [Walking the Way of Heaven].

The Secret Canon of Sitting in Oblivion, however, affected everything in the external world.

At advanced stages, through murmurs of the technique, one could reshape the entire world according to their own will.

For example, the technique contained a case:

A sign of small mastery of the Secret Canon of Sitting in Oblivion was to whisper and fabricate a person who never existed.

The more detailed the setting of this person became, the more they would actually come into being.

And they would act according to the predetermined rules.

Moreover, it would not sense the creator behind it.

The wonders of the Secret Canon of Sitting in Oblivion left Li Fan repeatedly in awe.

However, just like the Heaven and Earth Converging Manual, the Secret Canon of Sitting in Oblivion also had a severe side effect.

Every hundred years, a practitioner of this cultivation method would lose all memories except for the content of the Secret Canon of Sitting in Oblivion itself.

Any previously left written records or hints would be actively ignored by the practitioner.

In other words, even if one cultivated the Secret Canon of Sitting in Oblivion to its highest level, the entire world could be their creation.

Yet the cultivator would remain completely unaware.

Every hundred years, a new life would begin.

...

“To invent the Secret Canon of Sitting in Oblivion would require truly world-shaking talent,” Li Fan murmured, then turned to the final cultivation method.

The Three Perils of Ghosts and Spirits in the Netherworld Record.

This cultivation method was the most unusual.

It was a summoning technique.

But it did not summon beings from other worlds. Instead, it summoned the Ghosts and Gods within the human body itself.

According to the method’s explanation, every person is born with endless thoughts.

These thoughts are like waves on the sea, fleeting and ephemeral.

Yet it is these countless waves that form the vast sea known as the Human.

If every thought that arises is preserved, nurtured, and strengthened day and night,

Then hundreds of millions of thought essences would form within one’s Sea of Consciousness.

These essences take shape according to the nature and content of the original thought, becoming the Ghosts and Gods.

The so-called “Three Perils” refer to these three: Human, Ghost, and God.

Because these Ghosts and Gods originate from oneself, all three are interconnected.

Furthermore, as the strength of the Human increases, the power of the Ghosts and Gods multiplies hundreds or thousands of times.

A single thought released would seem as if the Netherworld itself had returned to the mortal realm.

Darkness would spread, and souls would run rampant.

If deliberately unleashed, it would be easy for one thought to destroy the entire world, transforming all other living beings into one’s own Ghosts and Gods.

This Three Perils of Ghosts and Spirits in the Netherworld Record did not have any particularly strong side effects.

If one had to mention a drawback, it would be the constant whispering of countless, never-ending Ghosts and Gods within the body.

If the mind is not firm, one could truly drive oneself insane.

Even with a strong will, facing the ceaseless murmurs of billions of Ghosts and Gods would eventually become unbearable.

When that moment came, the practitioner would lose control.

Billions of Ghosts and Gods could be unleashed to destroy the world.

However, when combined with the Secret Canon of Sitting in Oblivion, the results were unexpectedly beneficial.

The memory deletion every hundred years would sweep away all the negative emotions accumulated from the billions of Ghosts and Gods.

Yet the Ghosts and Gods themselves would not disappear.

Even if one could no longer remember the Three Perils of Ghosts and Spirits in the Netherworld Record, the cultivation of Ghost and God essences would have already become an instinct of the body.

In this way, as time passed, one’s strength would continue to grow.

“However, I remember Sun Xingwang mentioning that cultivating these two methods together has other unexpected points of conflict,” Li Fan thought.

“It is not as perfect as one might imagine.”

Chapter 930: Holy Blood Reforms the Embryo

Which cultivation method should he practice?

Li Fan hesitated for a moment, uncertain.

All of these were indeed genuine Immortal methods, impressive in their own right, but...

A shadow flickered across Li Fan’s eyes.

In the first illusion he experienced under Senior Sister Zhao, he had once practiced two methods: The Heaven and Earth Converging Manual and Soul-Severing Divine Radiance Technique.

Beyond the characteristics of the methods themselves, they shared a very special feature.

Once cultivated, they could never be discarded.

Even using the secret techniques of the Taishang Sect or the original scriptures of the Taishang Sect could not bypass this.

Moreover, there was an even more fatal point.

All methods of the Xuantian Sect seemed to carry a special mark. True experts, no matter how well hidden, could be recognized at a glance by those in the know.

The Xuantian Sect even possessed a secret method called [Seize Heaven and Replace It], which could not only completely strip away a method or cultivation, but also transfer the realm and strength gained from practicing a Xuantian Sect method as if performing a miraculous substitution.

At that time, he was merely an ordinary cultivator who had accidentally entered the illusion; Senior Sister Zhao had no reason to deliberately alter information regarding the Xuantian Sect.

Therefore, it could basically be considered true.

The strange part was here:

From the soul fragments of Ji Yuzhen, Li Fan had found no related memories.

Combined with the near-perfect persona displayed by Xuantian King...

It sent a chill down Li Fan’s spine.

This was also a major reason why Li Fan had always been suspicious of Xuantian King’s “good person” identity.

Of course, it was also possible that Ji Yuzhen’s soul was incomplete, causing some memories to be lost.

But in any case, Li Fan had to remain cautious regarding these Xuantian Sect methods.

After all, these were not unique copies he had personally obtained. They had been passed down by Xuantian King and then transferred through Senior Sister Zhao.

Given their cultivation levels, if they had tampered with the methods, Li Fan would have no way of noticing.

“My objective is merely to make it appear convincing,” he thought.

...

After a moment of contemplation, Li Fan came up with an idea.

“Perhaps I can use the Derivative Law Jade to derive a method that looks similar externally but differs in essence.”

“This carries some risk, but I can employ the Holy Blood Embryo Transformation method to create a clone and try it out.”

“If it fails, only my true self will have to risk practicing it.”

“However, after the next use of [Truth], cultivation cannot be retained.”

Li Fan’s choice may have seemed cautious to the extreme, but he knew well that when contending with the old monsters of the Xuanhuang Realm, no amount of caution was excessive.

A single misstep could easily plant the seed of ultimate failure.

Of course, regardless of the outcome, this trip to the Grand Dao Sect was far from unproductive.

Even if he could not immediately devote himself fully to cultivation, first, the True Immortal methods had expanded his horizons, showing him that cultivation techniques could indeed be so mysterious.

Second...

When Li Fan’s strength gradually grew in the future and he uncovered the secrets of the Xuantian Sect, these methods could truly become resources for his cultivation.

Taking a deep breath, he temporarily set these methods aside in the depths of his mind.

Li Fan then followed his plan and directly activated the Holy Blood Embryo Transformation Method.

The materials he needed had all been leftover from his last scavenging in the Empire. Not long after, a body extremely similar to Li Fan’s appeared before him.

The clone, Li Qing, had no memory of the “Holy Blood Embryo Transformation” technique, and a trace of surprise appeared in his eyes.

As usual, Li Fan had modified the memories and soul of the Holy Embryo.

With previous experience, his movements became increasingly skillful and smooth.

Before long, the Holy Embryo Li Fan opened its eyes.

The greatest weakness of the Holy Blood Embryo Transformation technique was that the resulting Holy Fetus was only a mortal body, possessing no cultivation.

It required cultivation step by step.

Without a prodigious talent like Mr. Bai, it would seem quite useless.

But in this world, with the existence of the Faceless Statue, Li Fan could easily resolve this drawback.

Grasping the Holy Embryo, Li Fan instantly appeared before the Faceless Statue.

As usual, countless visions surged like a tidal wave.

However, the strongest and most genuine among them was not Su Yuqing.

It was He Zhenghao!

“True Immortal of Salvation and Mortal Compassion, above! Please bless me to come up with more content for the Xuantian Spirit-Sealing Formation...”

He Zhenghao prayed constantly in his heart, extremely devout.

From the constantly flashing visions, Li Fan immediately understood what had recently happened to him.

Just as Li Fan had anticipated, on that day, he had used the Faceless Statue to transmit the contents of the Xuantian Spirit-Sealing Formation into He Zhenghao’s mind.

At the headquarters of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance’s Array Formation Hall, when He Zhenghao casually presented his various conjectures about the Xuantian Spirit-Sealing Formation, he immediately drew great attention.

He himself was regarded as a rare talent in array formation, and his treatment and circumstances instantly changed.

He Zhenghao also continuously reported the inspirations that surfaced in his mind in hopes of attaining higher status.

But problems soon arose.

Although He Zhenghao’s talent in formation was considerable, it was still far from independently deriving an array of the Xuantian Spirit-Sealing Formation’s level.

Even if he understood the complete formation diagram, the true essence of the formation remained elusive to him.

He Zhenghao was also somewhat shrewd, not submitting the full contents all at once.

He would occasionally improve some parts, trying to delay as much as possible.

He hoped to study the Xuantian Spirit-Sealing Formation through his own effort.

However, as days passed, the slow-progressing He Zhenghao eventually realized the truth.

The chasm between him and the Xuantian Spirit-Sealing Formation was far too vast and deep.

Relying solely on himself, it was impossible to comprehend the formation’s true essence.

Thus, he placed his only hope in the True Immortal of Salvation and Mortal Compassion.

Every day, though he appeared to be diligently deriving the formation, in reality, his mind repeatedly prayed.

His devotion exceeded that of all other members of the Alliance.

Li Fan, understanding the situation in an instant, shook his head slightly.

However, since He Zhenghao was a familiar figure, and to attract Mo Rubin’s attention he still needed him as a pawn, Li Fan would not let him die.

With a thought, a vast amount of annotations and explanations regarding the Xuantian Spirit-Sealing Formation were transmitted ceaselessly into He Zhenghao’s mind.

The sheer volume of data was so overwhelming that He Zhenghao momentarily could not endure it and fell into a deep, hazy sleep.

There was nothing noteworthy with the other disciples. The Holy Embryo Li Fan looked toward the Faceless Statue.

Like other members of the Alliance, it began to pray sincerely.

Not long after, Li Fan sensed a mysterious connection, a transfer completed through the Faceless Statue, establishing a link between the original and the Holy Embryo.

Li Fan squinted. In the next moment, a large amount of cultivation was poured into the Holy Embryo’s body.

The originally mortal body of the Holy Embryo underwent a tremendous and transformative change in an instant.

Chapter 931: Battle Needs No Words

Within a few breaths, the Holy Embryo of Li Fan rapidly ascended, rising from an ordinary mortal directly to the early stage of Qi Condensation.

Completely ignoring the existence of the Calamity of Heaven, it continued to advance toward the perfection stage of Qi Condensation.

It only stopped because of the absence of a suitable Foundation Establishment treasure.

This time, no special rare treasure was chosen.

Instead, Li Fan’s true body and his clone worked together, using Sword Dao to create a lower-grade Human-level Wonder item out of thin air, which served as the material for the Holy Embryo’s advancement.

With the "Unyielding Sword" as its Dao Foundation, the Holy Embryo was able to continue receiving Li Fan’s immense cultivation.

Transforming Sword Dao into the Golden Core method was already a familiar process.

As for the Cave Heaven required for the Holy Embryo to advance to the Nascent Soul stage, Li Fan directly cut off a small portion from his own Five Elements Grand Cave Heaven.

Thus, in only three days, another early-stage Nascent Soul True Lord-level clone was successfully created.

However, compared to Li Qing, this Holy Embryo was pitifully weak.

It only possessed the cultivation realm in name; if it were to fight, it might not even be a match for an ordinary Nascent Soul cultivator of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

But as a disposable pawn for executing dangerous missions, it was more than sufficient.

After preparing all the supplies for the Holy Embryo, Li Fan controlled it to unconsciously fly out of the Spiritwood Realm.

Only after traveling half a state away from the entrance did it fully awaken.

When the mission requirements emerged in its mind, the Holy Embryo began calmly heading toward its destination.

At this moment, Li Fan looked toward Li Qing.

“The mark of the new defensive grand formation left on your body, I have already erased it. However, do not go out for now. Focus solely on refining your Sword Dao.”

“I have a premonition that the chaos of the Xuanhuang Realm will soon begin again.”

Li Qing nodded slightly.

Li Fan’s words were not without reason.

From He Zhenghao’s memories, he had just seen a familiar figure.

Gongsun Qixuan of the Tianji Hall of Congyun Sea.

This person’s cultivation was not high, and his knowledge of formations was not particularly deep.

Yet he appeared at the headquarters of the Formation Strategy Hall of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

This was indeed very suspicious.

Li Fan recalled that in the 115th lifetime, when the Mysterious-Net underwent mutation, it was precisely this person who served as the catalyst.

Li Fan could almost conclude that there was a subtle connection between this person and the Tianxuan Mirror.

“Let them fight. The fiercer, the better.”

“Let me see what trump cards you all truly have.”

Li Fan’s gaze seemed to pierce countless layers of space, landing upon the border between the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and the Five Elders Association.

Within the Xuanhuang Realm, cultivators like Li Fan, those eagerly awaiting the clash of these two colossal powers—were truly rare.

Most cultivators only wished to live their lives peacefully.

They had never even imagined that the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and the Five Elders Association would engage in a full-scale war.

After all, once war began, countless lives would be lost.

Without irreconcilable hatred, why would anyone wish to fight?

Yet, on this day, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance suddenly found an excuse to declare war.

“The Five Elders Association must immediately return the lost treasure of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. If you refuse, the Immortal Alliance army will personally come to reclaim it.”

...

When the Immortal Alliance cultivators read this abrupt and somewhat inexplicable announcement, they all looked at each other in confusion.

“What treasure of the Immortal Alliance? How could it end up with the Five Elders Association?”

“This excuse seems far too clumsy.”

Just as the cultivators were puzzled, a detailed explanation was soon released—

It turned out that this was not a baseless accusation at all.

Back when the Five Elders Association was still known as the Ten Great Immortal Sects, they once launched a cruel and inhumane extermination campaign against the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

At that time, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had only just been established. Many of its core high-ranking members were slaughtered miserably.

It was said that even many of the alliance’s secret treasures fell into the hands of the Five Elders Association.

Although a great calamity later descended, causing the Ten Great Immortal Sects to collapse from within without even being attacked, and though thousands of years have since passed, the sea of blood and hatred between them has not faded even after a thousand generations!

To this day, the high command of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance has never forgotten the humiliation of that year. They have long been secretly accumulating strength, swearing to avenge that ancient shame!

Of course, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance claimed to be reasonable and not bent on total annihilation.

As long as the Five Elders Association would apologize for their deeds back then and return the secret treasures of the Immortal Alliance, the matter could be settled peacefully.

...

The public announcement quickly spread throughout the world. Regarding the authenticity of its contents, most cultivators were indifferent.

For anyone with eyes could tell that this was merely an excuse for the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance to launch a war.

Faced with this seemingly unreasonable provocation, the Five Elders Association was momentarily unable to respond.

It seemed that internal disputes had arisen, as no official response came from their side.

Thus, one full day after the announcement was made, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance launched its assault without hesitation.

Moreover, they appeared extremely confident in their strength.

Unlike before, this war was not kept secret.

Instead, it was broadcast in real time to all cultivators of the Immortal Alliance through the Tianxuan Mirror.

Of course, while it was called “real time,” there was still a brief delay.

Even so, that did not stop every cultivator of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance from gathering before the Tianxuan Mirror, excitedly watching the battle unfold.

The first to take the field was none other than the Immortal Ancestor’s Dharma-Manifestation, which had not appeared for quite some time.

Compared to the last time it was seen, the changes in the Dharma-Manifestation were visible to the naked eye.

Looking down at the land below, its gaze carried a trace of contempt.

Then it stretched out its right hand and pulled through the air toward the skies above the Immortal Alliance.

In that instant, over a thousand Sky-Patrolling Arrows that had been circulating within the alliance’s territory were all forcibly drawn before the Dharma-Manifestation.

Each Sky-Patrolling Arrow shone with a silver radiance, weaving together like fine threads to form an immense net.

With a slight motion, the Dharma-Manifestation swung its arm in a circular gesture, then hurled the great net toward the Five Elders Association.

Five rays of light, each of a different hue, instantly rose from various locations, attempting to block the descending net.

However...

The Dharma-Manifestation gave a low hum, and its form split into countless streams of light that shot fiercely toward the five rays.

The five lights faltered briefly under the collision with those radiant streams.

At that moment, another Immortal Ancestor’s Dharma-Manifestation quietly appeared!

It blocked the path of the Five Elders Association’s purple ray of light head-on.

Boom!

A violent tremor shook the scene, and the Tianxuan Mirror’s image was momentarily cut off.

When the view was restored, what all the watching cultivators saw was a shocking sight:

Five Immortal Ancestor Dharma-Manifestations, each bearing a distinct expression, confronting five radiant beams of divine light head-on.

The entire Xuanhuang Realm seemed to tremble under the force of their collision.

Meanwhile, within the Spiritwood Realm, Li Fan had already faintly sensed something earlier.

He felt that Wei Qizheng, one of the Thirty-Three Chosen Immortal Experts, who was currently possessed by the Final Dissociation Disc, had his life force rapidly draining away!

Now, his oil was completely exhausted, his flame extinguished, and both body and Dao perished!

“This is...”

A gleam of light flashed in Li Fan’s eyes.

In his mind, the Final Dissociation Disc emitted a soft white glow.

It was receiving a massive influx of data—scenes of everything Wei Qizheng had experienced before his death.

“The fate of whether the Immortal Alliance can unify the Xuanhuang Realm... now rests entirely upon all of you.”

Chapter 932: The Residual Will of Immortal Ancestor

In Wei Qizheng’s memories, there was a middle-aged cultivator who spoke with passionate conviction. Although Li Fan had never met this man before, for some reason, the man’s tone, movements, and bearing all gave him a faint sense of familiarity.

Especially the fleeting cold glint in the man’s eyes—it made Li Fan almost certain that in one of his past lives, he must have crossed paths with this person.

While watching, Li Fan carefully observed the recorded scenes within the Dao Deviation Stone, searching for someone similar.

“The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and the Five Elders Association have stood in opposition for thousands of years, each gaining and losing in turn. Cultivators of the Xuanhuang Realm have been dragged into the chaos, suffering endlessly.”

“In order to end this cycle once and for all, countless predecessors exhausted their wisdom and effort, eventually devising a method so brilliant that it could be called peerless.”

As the middle-aged cultivator spoke, miniature projections of the various prefectures under the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance appeared before the young disciples.

“As you all know, within every floating Heavenly City of our Alliance stands a Immortal Ancestor Stone Statue. Most people believe these statues were erected merely to commemorate the great deeds of the Immortal Ancestor. But have you ever considered the possibility that these statues may serve another purpose?”

The moment these words left his mouth, all the young cultivators revealed astonished expressions.

“When you entered the headquarters of the Alliance, you must have sensed the lingering trace of the Immortal Ancestor’s aura.”

“These stone statues, too, were built under his direct guidance.”

“Even the locations of every floating city were personally designated by him.”

“Within each statue lies a residual fragment of the Immortal Ancestor’s will. If it were to be activated, it could summon the might equivalent to a single glance from the Immortal Ancestor...”

At this point, the middle-aged cultivator deliberately slowed his speech.

The young disciples, caught up in the moment, found the phrase ‘a single glance’ somewhat underwhelming.

No matter how powerful the Immortal Ancestor might have been, a mere glance—what could that possibly do?

Noticing their skeptical expressions, the cultivator smiled knowingly.

Then his face turned solemn, and his words resounded with power:

“A single glance from the Immortal Ancestor can withstand the full-force strike of an ordinary Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign!”

His voice echoed through the hall. The thirty-three cultivators present all widened their eyes in disbelief.

“How could that be possible?” one young cultivator could not help but cry out.

“No matter how strong the Immortal Ancestor was, he was still of the Longevity Immortal realm. How could two beings of the same realm differ so vastly in strength?”

“If the Immortal Ancestor was truly that powerful, why did he not simply unify the Xuanhuang Realm back then? Why leave behind such a chaotic mess?”

The hall instantly erupted in debate.

Ignoring the authority of the middle-aged cultivator, the youths began to argue fiercely.

But when the cultivator’s cold gaze swept across them, the familiar chill in his eyes caused silence to return, one by one.

“The Immortal Ancestor’s considerations are beyond our understanding,” he said coldly. “He must have had his reasons for not unifying the Xuanhuang Realm in those years. But now, times have changed. We have received the Immortal Ancestor’s clear directive.”

“It is time for the Xuanhuang Realm to have only one voice.”

His tone brimmed with killing intent.

“And you,” he said, his gaze sharp as a blade, “are the key to achieving this great mission.”

With a flick of his finger, the scene before them shifted once more.

The vast lands of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance vanished, leaving behind nearly a thousand points of light—each representing a floating Heavenly City.

Then, streams of silver light emerged, connecting those luminous points together.

“Do you see it now?” the middle-aged cultivator asked.

Wei Qizheng was the first to respond, blurting out in astonishment, “This... this looks exactly like the meridians of the human body!”

The other young cultivators all turned their gazes toward Wei Qizheng, and then fixed their eyes on the increasingly distinct web of intersecting lines ahead.

All of them suddenly came to a realization.

“Could it be...”

An unbelievable thought surged in their hearts.

The middle-aged cultivator spoke, confirming their suspicion. “The Immortal Ancestor’s Residual Will is distributed across the lands of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, following the special meridian patterns of the human body. If it can be activated through secret techniques, one can summon forth from the void a Dharma Manifestation modeled after the Immortal Ancestor.”

“The Residual Will of the Immortal Ancestor serves as the power source that drives the Manifestation.”

“If it can all be fully activated...”

The middle-aged cultivator took a deep breath. “Then the Five Elders Association could be annihilated within days!”

The thirty-three young cultivators were all stunned on the spot.

“And you,” the middle-aged cultivator continued, “are the keys to unlocking this immense power.”

As he spoke, the youths were all captivated. Their eyes shone with eagerness and excitement.

The power of the Immortal Ancestor’s Residual Will was far too vast. A single cultivator could never hope to fully activate or inherit it.

It had to be divided and distributed into thirty-three domains.

Each domain required an extraordinary genius to preside over it.

When these thirty-three chosen immortals could achieve complete unity of mind, and grow familiar with the Residual Will, they would together act as a lever to awaken the power of the Immortal Ancestor’s Residual Will.

“The first time you summon the Dharma Manifestation of the Immortal Ancestor, its duration will certainly not be long,” the middle-aged cultivator said solemnly.

“You must seize that opportunity to familiarize yourselves with and comprehend its profound mysteries.”

“Moreover, during this process, none of you may allow even the slightest distraction. You must understand that at that moment, it will be equivalent to all thirty-three of you controlling the Manifestation together.”

“If there is any discord among you, the Manifestation could collapse.”

The middle-aged cultivator thoroughly explained all matters regarding the Immortal Ancestor’s Dharma Manifestation.

These thirty-three cultivators were all proud and spirited young talents, each with his own arrogance.

To unite their hearts toward a single goal would be exceedingly difficult.

Therefore, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance prepared a long sequence of training for them.

Inside a transparent sphere filled with silvery-white light, countless rapidly evolving worlds seemed to exist.

The spiritual senses of the thirty-three were cast into these worlds, where they experienced countless trials.

There were chaotic mortal worlds.

There were turbulent martial realms.

There were adventures in the ancient cultivation world.

There were even visions of possible futures.

Yet without exception, all thirty-three were bound together by force, compelled to form bonds for various reasons and gather as one.

Only by achieving unity of heart and spirit, becoming as one entity, could they finally escape the cycle of reincarnation.

If even one among them harbored a divergent intent, the cycle would begin anew.

Even with the deliberate guidance of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, this was still an immensely long process.

No one knew how many years had passed within the virtual worlds before these “youths” finally emerged.

The youthful inexperience on their faces had vanished.

Their eyes were filled with the vicissitudes of time.

As they looked around, there was both vigilance and confusion in their expressions.

Clap, clap, clap...

With the sound of the middle-aged cultivator’s steady applause, their long-suppressed memories gradually resurfaced.

“Very good. Now we begin the second lesson,” the middle-aged cultivator said coldly, paying no heed to the young men’s state of mind.

...

After that, Wei Qizheng and the others underwent even harsher training.

They were stripped bare and confined within a sealed space of endless darkness.

There was nothing in that space except a simulated aura of the Immortal Ancestor.

Sight and hearing were completely deprived.

Chapter 933: The Fall of the Chosen Immortal

Even when their divine sense swept outward to explore the surroundings, all they could perceive was a boundless void.

Bathed day after day in the lingering aura of the Immortal Ancestor, the bodies and souls of the thirty-three Chosen Immortal Candidates gradually began to change.

In that endless darkness, no one knew how much time had passed.

When they finally escaped from their confinement, their expressions, actions, and even their eyes had all become eerily similar.

...

Seeing this, a flash of inspiration crossed Li Fan’s mind.

He finally remembered where that sense of familiarity toward the middle-aged cultivator had come from.

The cold glint in that man’s eyes was almost identical to that of the Diyi Jinglun.

“Just like those Chosen Immortal. Though they are not the same person, for some reason, they share the same experience, and thus come to resemble one another...”

“No wonder I could never find two identical faces.”

“Diyi Jinglun and this middle-aged cultivator are somewhat different from those black-robed cultivators who seem to have walked out of the river of time, and also from that one called [Wu]. Just how many secrets does the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance hide?”

A flood of thoughts flashed through Li Fan’s mind before he continued observing Wei Qizheng’s subsequent encounters.

...

After the second lesson ended, the final training began.

The thirty-three Chosen Immortal were each led to different provinces.

Within a special sealed space, they began to sense the lingering power hidden within the Immortal Ancestor’s statue.

With their previous training as a foundation, this goal had become somewhat easier to reach.

Of course, only relatively speaking.

Inside the enclosed space, this was the only task they could perform.

There even existed a mysterious power that guided their minds, making it impossible for them to think about anything else.

They could only sense day and night, hoping to achieve complete synchronization as soon as possible.

By this stage, the differences between the thirty-three Chosen Immortal were almost negligible.

They completed their synchronization nearly at the same time, with a margin of error no greater than two days.

All thirty-three had successfully attuned themselves to the residual will of the Immortal Ancestor.

Then, connected by a beam of pure light, their consciousnesses became linked together despite being in different parts of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

It was an extraordinarily wondrous sensation.

The thirty-three were as one whole; without speaking a word, they could communicate in an instant.

A single thought surpassed a thousand words.

Through their long-formed tacit understanding, their collective will became extremely unified, almost without fluctuation.

...

“Hm?” Li Fan’s thoughts were suddenly interrupted.

“What is this... this beam of pure light?”

“Why does it feel somewhat like Senior Sister Zhao’s One-Heart of All Beings?”

A trace of puzzlement flashed in Li Fan’s eyes.

As the one who knew the Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign, Senior Sister Zhao, best, he had personally experienced the peculiarity of her One-Heart, One Mind, more than once.

After repeatedly observing the scene of the pure light’s appearance dozens of times, Li Fan finally discerned something.

The thirty-three Chosen Immortal had achieved perfect unity of mind and thought, leaving no room for any other existence.

“This is merely an application of pure law power, not another One-Heart Heavenly Sovereign. Has Senior Sister Zhao’s power been stolen?” Li Fan frowned slightly.

“Does the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance truly possess such capability?”

Just as Li Fan had speculated, as the thirty-three Immortal Candidates’ wills continued merging, the pure light beam gradually faded away.

Eventually, even without any external aid, the thirty-three could directly share their thoughts.

After thousands of attempts, they finally managed to draw upon the immense power left behind by the Immortal Ancestor.

Because of the middle-aged cultivator’s statement—“A single gaze from the Immortal Ancestor equals one strike from a Longevity Immortal Realm cultivator”—the impact it left upon them was immense.

Thus, when the thirty-three Chosen Immortal unanimously made their choice, the first manifestation of the Immortal Ancestor’s Dharma Manifestation was titled "Opening Eyes to Flatten Nine Mountains."

After their first success, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance finally allowed them to rest for a short while.

However, this so-called rest merely meant they were given a bit of free time.

They still had to remain within that special space and were forbidden from leaving.

After a brief period of respite, they began another round of attempts.

With each trial, they became increasingly skilled, and the Immortal Ancestor power they invoked grew ever stronger.

Until that day came...

When the Immortal Ancestor’s manifestation seized the Sky-Patrolling Arrow and wove it into a massive net, which was then hurled toward the territory of the Five Elders Association, it finally provoked the intervention of the Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns of the Five Elders Association.

According to the plan, the thirty-three Chosen Immortals immediately detonated the manifestation, unleashing its instantaneous power to the limit.

They sought to obstruct the five Heavenly Sovereigns and ensure that the giant net would descend upon its target.

And they succeeded.

Although exhausted, a surge of pride arose within their hearts.

To be below the Dao Integration Realm, yet capable of directly confronting the five most powerful Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns of the Xuanhuang Realm—what an incomparable honor that was.

Yet, before they could even celebrate, a new command resounded.

“Continue evolving the manifestation.”

All thirty-three Chosen Immortals trembled inwardly.

Even now, invoking the manifestation of the Immortal Ancestor was no easy task for them.

It consumed an immense amount of spiritual and mental energy, requiring a long time to recover afterward.

But that mysterious voice carried a strange power.

It compelled the thirty-three Chosen Immortals, leaving them utterly unable to resist.

They instinctively obeyed.

Another manifestation appeared.

Before they could rest, that cold and emotionless voice sounded again.

“Continue.”

The thirty-three Chosen Immortals realized something was wrong.

If this continued, they would all surely die from over-consumption.

Yet they could not disobey the voice’s command.

Command after command followed.

Eventually, five Heavenly Sovereign manifestations appeared simultaneously between heaven and earth.

Because their essence, energy, and spirit had been drained in such a short time, the thirty-three Chosen Immortals reached the end of their lives.

“Why?”

“Were we not the hope of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance’s revival?”

“Were we not heroes?”

In the final moments of their lives, their minds were filled with confusion and resentment.

Yet, until their consciousness completely sank into darkness, not a single person came forward to explain anything.

Not a single word.

Not even mockery.

...

“The world says that I, Grand Preceptor Li, am a great villain.”

“I once believed that myself.”

“But today, I finally understand...”

“Compared to the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, I still have much to learn.”

After witnessing everything that Wei Qizheng had experienced, Li Fan could not help but sigh.

Back in reality, the confrontation between the Five Immortal Ancestor Dharma-Manifestations and the five Heavenly Sovereigns continued to shake the Xuanhuang Realm more and more violently.

The battlefield seemed momentarily frozen.

Five radiant lights tangled with the Heavenly Sovereign manifestations, locked in fierce struggle.

The manifestations, summoned through the life essence of the thirty-three Chosen Immortals, were far stronger than those that had appeared before.

Even under the combined assault of the five Heavenly Sovereigns, their bodies shattered yet refused to disperse.

At last, the giant Sky-Patrolling Arrow Net descended upon the territory of the Five Elders Association.

The image within the Tianxuan Mirror changed once again.

Chapter 934: The Setback Of The Five Heavenly Sovereigns

After the vast web of the Sky-Patrolling Arrows broke through the obstruction of the Heavenly Sovereigns, it instantly dispersed once again into nearly ten thousand silver-white fine arrows.

These arrows swiftly flew toward every prefectures under the jurisdiction of the Five Elders Association.

Although many cultivators of the Five Elders Association tried to intercept them, the sheer number of these Sky-Patrolling Arrows was simply overwhelming.

Moreover, each arrow carried power equivalent to a single strike from a Dao Integration expert. The incident happened so suddenly that even the Dao Integration cultivators of the Five Elders Association found it extremely difficult to stop them in time.

Thus, those ten thousand Sky-Patrolling Arrows, like a dazzling display of fireworks, split into countless trajectories and fell upon the lands of the Five Elders Association.

Booming explosions echoed endlessly.

Even through the Tianxuan Mirror, Li Fan could feel the massive tremors spreading from afar.

However, the Sky-Patrolling Arrow web that the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had unleashed, at the cost of the lives of all thirty-three of its chosen immortals—was far from a mere bombardment.

After a brief silence, from every place where the Sky-Patrolling Arrows had landed, tens of thousands of silver threads suddenly erupted outward.

They formed enormous cocoons of light, wide at the base and narrow at the top, resembling strange clamps rooted deep into the earth.

From the tips of those cocoons, countless thin threads shot toward the direction of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

In an instant, spanning tens of millions of miles, nearly ten thousand silver threads converged together within the territory of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

All these changes happened in the blink of an eye, before even the five remaining Heavenly Sovereigns’ Manifestation in the sky had completely shattered.

Watching this scene through the Tianxuan Mirror, even with his countless lifetimes of experience, Li Fan could not comprehend what the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance was attempting to do.

Yet within his heart, a faint and ominous sense of foreboding began to rise.

Under the gaze of countless beings, it was as though a colossal giant stood upon the side of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and gave a mighty pull.

A thunderous roar erupted once more.

The tens of thousands of cocoons deeply embedded within the earth of the Five Elders Association’s territories tightened like resilient ropes, as if dragging or drawing something toward the direction of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

The rumbling continued endlessly, as if some monumental change was occurring between heaven and earth.

Meanwhile, the five radiant lights that were opposing the remaining Heavenly Sovereigns’ Dharma Manifestation seemed to grow even more frantic.

But strangely, their attacks weakened instead of intensifying.

The projections of the Heavenly Sovereigns, which were on the verge of collapsing completely, somehow managed to endure for a longer time.

Li Fan’s sense of unease deepened.

After carefully reflecting on it, he realized that it was not a fear concerning his own safety. Rather, it felt like a primal instinct shared by all living beings, similar to the anxiety that precedes a natural catastrophe.

“This is…”

Within the Tianxuan Mirror, it appeared that the Five Elders Association had finally realized the severity of the situation.

They dispatched a massive number of cultivators to attack the enormous cocoons of light.

Yet, these cocoons seemed almost intangible; most attacks simply passed through them without effect.

Only a handful of leaders, those of Dao Integration level—were able to slightly distort the silver threads emitted from the cocoons.

However, the massive web of light enveloping the entire Five Elders Association region was incredibly resilient. Those faint distortions barely affected the overall structure.

The changes continued, firm and unstoppable.

In the heavens, the light representing the five Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereigns grew increasingly dim.

It was as if… the powers of these Heavenly Sovereigns were being restricted by some mysterious force.

At that moment, a sudden flash of insight struck Li Fan’s mind.

It was as if lightning had split through the mist; in his vision, the scene changed drastically.

The light cocoons and connecting threads no longer linked to the land of the Five Elders Association but instead to an enormous web—

A colossal web representing the principles of Heaven and Earth!

Li Fan finally understood.

Each Heavenly Sovereign of the Five Elders Association had their own territory.

Within these regions, the way they reversed the principles of Heaven and Earth was far more stable than in the outside world.

Even the weaker ones, if they left their regions, would lose the ability to reverse the principles of Heaven and Earth.

These areas could even be considered the places where these Heavenly Sovereigns achieved their Dao.

Now, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, through some unknown method, had used the light cocoons to distort the inherent principles of Heaven and Earth in these regions.

It was like the pillars supporting a bridge bending.

Although the bridge would not collapse immediately, it began to sway uncontrollably.

In reality, this meant that the powers of the five Heavenly Sovereigns were inevitably affected.

“This will not affect the strength of the Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign, but it can temporarily make them lose absolute control over the territories of the Five Elders Association.”

Li Fan stood up, astonishment written across his face.

He had never imagined that such a method could be used when fighting the Longevity Immortal Heavenly Sovereign.

“The distortion of the principles of Heaven and Earth is temporary. The five Heavenly Sovereigns will undoubtedly find a way to slowly eliminate this effect. But before that happens, this is the perfect opportunity for the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance…”

As if responding to Li Fan’s thoughts, portals suddenly appeared above the Five Elders Association.

A large number of black-clad cultivators flew out of the gates.

Expressionless, exuding murderous intent.

They began sweeping through the various regions of the Five Elders Association.

“Heavenly Artificers…”

Li Fan was already familiar with these elite soldiers trained by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance over generations.

Their formidable combat power remained as overwhelming as ever.

The situation was too sudden. With the Five Elders Association in chaos, there was no time to organize any meaningful resistance.

Fighting individually, they were quickly defeated one by one by the black-clad cultivators.

The assault of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance was like a heavy, unstoppable cloud pressing down on the Five Elders Association.

In front of the Tianxuan Mirror, cultivators who had been watching casually went into a frenzy.

“What is this? What is happening? Is this still the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance I know?”

“The Immortal Alliance has hidden so much. Their combat forces are unimaginably powerful!”

“Could it be… just a possibility, mind you… that these images are illusions created by the Tianxuan Mirror? After all, the opposing side has been facing us for thousands of years. Could they really be defeated so easily? I find it hard to believe.”

“My thoughts exactly. It seems unreasonable. Is there anyone brave enough to go to the Five Elders Association personally and see? Seeing is believing.”

“Hehe, I already know a secret route to the Five Elders Association. Friends, wait a moment. I’ll go and verify it myself.”

“I spit on you! You rebels, still so arrogant. I want to see your shocked faces when the Five Elders Association is completely overrun!”

…

In front of the Tianxuan Mirror, the human world displayed all manners of reactions.

Yet the battle between the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and the Five Elders Association did not shift according to their wishes.

The black-clad cultivators seemed endless, continuously flying out of the portals.

They required no orders, yet acted with exceptional coordination.

After leaving the portals, each moved decisively and efficiently.

They occupied key positions, built defensive fortifications, and steadily advanced.

They did not slaughter indiscriminately but instead captured all the Five Elders Association cultivators they encountered, transporting them through the portals.

Previous

Next

They began sweeping through the various regions of the Five Elders Association.

“Heavenly Artificers…”

Li Fan was already familiar with these elite soldiers trained by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance over generations.

Their formidable combat power remained as overwhelming as ever.

The situation was too sudden. With the Five Elders Association in chaos, there was no time to organize any meaningful resistance.

Fighting individually, they were quickly defeated one by one by the black-clad cultivators.

The assault of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance was like a heavy, unstoppable cloud pressing down on the Five Elders Association.

In front of the Tianxuan Mirror, cultivators who had been watching casually went into a frenzy.

“What is this? What is happening? Is this still the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance I know?”

“The Immortal Alliance has hidden so much. Their combat forces are unimaginably powerful!”

“Could it be… just a possibility, mind you… that these images are illusions created by the Tianxuan Mirror? After all, the opposing side has been facing us for thousands of years. Could they really be defeated so easily? I find it hard to believe.”

“My thoughts exactly. It seems unreasonable. Is there anyone brave enough to go to the Five Elders Association personally and see? Seeing is believing.”

“Hehe, I already know a secret route to the Five Elders Association. Friends, wait a moment. I’ll go and verify it myself.”

“I spit on you! You rebels, still so arrogant. I want to see your shocked faces when the Five Elders Association is completely overrun!”

…

In front of the Tianxuan Mirror, the human world displayed all manners of reactions.

Yet the battle between the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and the Five Elders Association did not shift according to their wishes.

The black-clad cultivators seemed endless, continuously flying out of the portals.

They required no orders, yet acted with exceptional coordination.

After leaving the portals, each moved decisively and efficiently.

They occupied key positions, built defensive fortifications, and steadily advanced.

They did not slaughter indiscriminately but instead captured all the Five Elders Association cultivators they encountered, transporting them through the portals.

Chapter 937: The Soul Fragments Of The Dharma Transmitter

However, the spy network of the Five Elders Association also seemed to have suffered significant disruption.

No one came to receive him.

Li Qing had no choice but to continue deeper until he reached a region called Fanqing Prefecture, where he finally received a response.

Yet, when the connection was made, the other side remained silent—waiting for Li Qing to speak first.

“I am a member of the Federation of All Worlds. I have an urgent matter and must contact your superior,” Li Qing transmitted through his spiritual voice.

“Federation of All Worlds? What is that?” came a voice that sounded startled, as if awakening from a dream.

The voice was clearly young.

After a burst of static and muffled noise, the contact was replaced by a calm, middle-aged cultivator.

“Please forgive me, Daoist friend. The world is in chaos now. Many key figures in the organization disappeared during the great battle, their life or death uncertain. Therefore, at the moment, we cannot confirm your identity…”

It was a direct refusal to meet.

After a short silence, Li Qing spoke again. “Precisely because of this chaos, key intelligence is even more valuable. The information I hold could turn the tide of the Five Elders Association’s dire situation.”

“A merit of great magnitude—are you willing to let it slip by for nothing? Many positions are now vacant. If you achieve something significant, promotion will be within reach.”

“Besides, the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance already holds an overwhelming advantage. Do you still think I am one of their spies?”

His tone was laced with mockery. In just a few sentences, he pierced straight through the man’s mental defenses.

The sound of breathing on the other end grew rapid.

Then the communication abruptly ended.

Li Qing was in no hurry. He waited patiently, full of confidence.

Sure enough, less than half a day later, the same middle-aged cultivator took the initiative to reach out again.

“Where shall we meet?”

“But let me make this clear first—I came here on my own. If your information turns out unreliable, I’ll pretend we never met.”

Li Qing smiled faintly and gave him his location.

Not long after, a streak of black light shot across the sky and descended before her.

A cultivator wrapped head to toe in metallic armor, his face completely obscured, appeared silently before Li Qing.

“Quite an outfit,” Li Qing remarked.

The armor was identical to that worn by the cultivators who had flown out from the floating city in the sky.

“At times like this, Carefree Paradise finally lets us use such equipment,” the middle-aged man said curtly, his tone filled with resentment. “If they hadn’t hoarded these things all along, would we have been defeated so miserably?”

As he spoke, a transparent ripple pulsed from his armor, spreading outward to form a circular barrier that enclosed them both.

The surroundings blurred—the barrier completely isolated sound and sight from the outside.

“All right. It’s safe here now. What is this important information you spoke of?” the man asked tensely, fixing his gaze on Li Qing.

Li Qing smiled faintly. “I told you, it’s information that could reverse the tide of war. How could I simply give it away?”

The middle-aged cultivator grew anxious. “Then what do you want?”

“At the very least, I want to speak to someone with real authority,” Li Qing said firmly.

“If you don’t say anything, how can I tell you’re not lying? In times like these, you expect me to bring an unidentified person to see my superior? Am I looking for death?” the man snapped.

It was obvious he was not trained in intelligence or espionage—he lacked subtlety and restraint, showing his irritation openly.

Li Qing, seeing the situation, decided not to waste any more time arguing. He spoke directly. “Our Federation of All Worlds knows how to break the Immortal Ancestor Manifestation of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance. What do you think of that?”

“What?” The middle-aged cultivator froze for a moment, then blurted out in disbelief.

“Is that true?” His voice trembled slightly.

“I will wait here,” Li Qing said coldly, “until someone of authority arrives.”

The middle-aged cultivator lowered his head and pondered for a moment before finally gritting his teeth.

Without showing any fluctuation of divine sense, he completed the notification.

Before such weighty information, the Five Elders Association finally displayed the level of efficiency they were expected to have.

In the time it took for half a cup of tea, over a hundred streaks of black light flew toward the area.

They were all clad in metal battle armor and surrounded Li Qing in formation.

To his surprise, the one leading them was a female cultivator. She walked straight up to Li Qing. “A friend from the Federation of All Worlds?”

Li Qing nodded in acknowledgment, confirming his identity.

“My name is Qing Yang,” the woman said. “I am responsible for rebuilding the postwar intelligence system. Follow me.”

Encircled by more than a hundred cultivators, Li Qing was led into an underground fortress.

His expression remained perfectly calm throughout.

That composure earned him a measure of trust from the members of the Five Elders Association.

The underground fortress had clearly been built recently, a precaution against attacks from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance.

Reaching the deepest chamber, Qing Yang dismissed the others, leaving only herself and Li Qing face to face.

“Speak. You can say whatever you came for now.” Qing Yang’s tone was devoid of emotion.

Li Qing gave a faint smile. “Miss Qing Yang, you do not even dare show your face. That makes it hard for me to trust you.”

After a brief silence, the black metal armor dissolved, revealing Qing Yang’s true appearance.

Her features were plain, the sort one would easily overlook in a crowd.

Yet Li Qing keenly sensed the indifference to life reflected in the woman’s eyes.

After a long and careful observation, Li Qing finally spoke. “We will exchange intelligence on the Chosen Immortals for the information we seek.”

“Agreed,” Qing Yang replied immediately.

“What is it that you wish to know?” she asked decisively.

“What exactly is the Immortal Alliance Treasure that the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance is after?”

“Hm?” Qing Yang was clearly taken aback by the question, momentarily stunned.

“This... I am not sure. But it should not be classified. I will inquire.”

The metallic light once again covered her face.

When her features reappeared, they were filled with visible doubt and confusion.

“This information is above my clearance level...”

Now she looked unsettled.

“Apologies. Please wait a moment,” Qing Yang said, before hastily leaving the chamber.

Li Qing fell into deep thought.

“So it is true. That so-called Immortal Alliance Relic, which sounded like an excuse, truly exists.”

“What sort of treasure did the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance once possess, that it could haunt them for thousands of years?”

As he pondered, Qing Yang soon returned.

Her expression was strange as she spoke. “The so-called Immortal Alliance Relic refers to the remnants of the first-generation members of the Alliance—fragments of their souls and their corpses.”

“In the past, when the Immortal Sects besieged the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance as a warning to the world, they extracted the souls of the defeated Dharma-Transmitter and tortured them endlessly.”

“At the time, such cruelty was considered normal, even publicly celebrated. But in retrospect, it is far from glorious, so the matter has been kept secret.”

In the Spiritwood Realm, Li Fan, who sensed this information through his clone, narrowed his eyes.

He remembered that during the 115th lifetime, the Five Elders Association had launched a sudden raid on Ten Thousand Immortal Island, apparently also to unearth the remains of those first-generation Dharma-Transmitters.

Chapter 938: Victory And Defeat Remain Unknown

“On different timelines, both sides once went to war over the remains of the First Generation Dharma-Transmitter. It seems that beyond their symbolic meaning, these remains must have another purpose.”

In that instant, Li Fan felt a strong urge to raid the Ten Thousand Immortal Island.

But he quickly suppressed that thought by force.

Though on the surface, Ten Thousand Immortal Island in the Congyun Sea seemed a remote region, its strongest resident cultivators only at the Soul Transformation Realm, existences that Li Fan could now easily suppress with a wave of his hand—there was more to it.

Beneath Ten Thousand Immortal Island lay the Nether Passage, said to be the possible source of this Xuanhuang Realm’s strange anomalies.

And Gongsun Qixuan, the supervisor from the Tianji Hall responsible for monitoring the anomalies, was certainly not as simple as he appeared to be.

To dig beneath the eyes of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance…

“Actually, it’s not impossible. As long as I stir the waters once more.”

“But there’s still something strange. If they wanted the remains, why not just dig them directly from Ten Thousand Immortal Island? Why insist on taking the batch seized by the Five Elders Association?”

A trace of doubt flashed through Li Fan’s mind.

“It seems I’ll need to contact Mo Rubin as soon as possible.”

The scene then shifted—to Li Fan’s clone, Li Qing, within the Five Elders Association.

“The remains? The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance made such a grand move just for those?” Li Qing voiced the same question as his true self.

“Yes.” Qing Yang once again confirmed.

“In the Soul-Grinding Plate, there are fifty-six fragmented souls and one hundred and three sets of remains.”

“However, after so many years of torment, they’ve already gone completely insane. They should have lost all value.” Qing Yang gave Li Qing a glance, as if intentionally or not.

Li Qing’s face showed no change. He pretended to ponder for a moment, then said, “Since you’re being so forthright, I’ll also be direct.”

“All thirty-three Chosen Immortal have died. The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance won’t be able to summon the Immortal Ancestor's Manifestation again anytime soon.”

“Moreover, the previous batch of Chosen Immortal Candidates was chosen through the massive public trials of the Alliance Grand Examination. To recruit replacements will take time. That gap might just be your window to strike back.” Li Qing didn’t say too much—just quietly sowed a seed of discord.

Upon hearing this, Qing Yang remained outwardly calm, but the flicker in her eyes betrayed the shock within.

“I wonder when the Five Heavenly Sovereigns will fully recover their power?” Li Qing seized the chance to ask.

Qing Yang shook her head and refused to speak.

“A pity,” Li Qing sighed softly. “I was hoping to renew my acquaintance with One-Heart Heavenly Sovereign.”

Qing Yang’s eyes suddenly sharpened, her killing intent almost tangible.

Unfazed, Li Qing cupped his hands slightly. “My mission is complete. I’ll take my leave.”

“Hopefully, we’ll have a chance to work together again in the future.”

Li Qing turned to leave, and Qing Yang did not stop him.

Before departing, they exchanged a new contact method. Then Li Qing transformed into a streak of sword light and vanished into the sky.

“Milord, that one’s behavior is suspicious. You’re just letting him go?” A shadowy figure emerged behind Qing Yang, speaking in a low tone.

“Although his strength isn’t weak, I could easily keep him here if I wished.”

Qing Yang’s gaze lingered on the distant sky. Only after the last trace of sword light faded did she speak softly, “He is not our enemy. For now, let him be.” ᚱαΝỘᛒË𝙨

“How goes the Poison Seed Project?”

The shadow answered in a grave voice, “Still no contact. The Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance seems to have captured our people. Their whereabouts are unknown.”

“Keep tracking them,” Qing Yang said, her eyes glinting coldly.

“Since they dared to extend their hand, they must be prepared to have it severed.”

“Yes!” The shadow’s tone carried a hint of excitement.

…

Li Qing did not know what had happened after he left.

However, he could faintly sense a trace of malice lingering around him, belonging to that shadowy presence.

Because of that, he did not linger. After obtaining the result he wanted, he left the territory of the Five Elders Association.

But on his way back, he happened to witness a scene that deeply shocked him.

Amid the vast ruins that the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance had nearly razed to the ground, a group of cultivators appeared, each wearing a set of radiant, flowing battle armor.

They hovered in midair, and from the heads of their armor, beams of light projected downward, forming the phantom outlines of buildings upon the land.

As the light beams grew brighter and more solid, those once-illusory structures gradually became real, shifting from illusion to substance.

Within less than half a cup of tea’s time, the group of cultivators had effortlessly conjured an entire city out of thin air.

“Task complete. Let’s see the reward this time.”

“This is way too stingy... whatever, on to the next one.”

They muttered as they flew off one after another.

“How interesting.” Li Qing narrowed his eyes slightly.

The cultivators from the Carefree Paradise seemed entirely different from those of the outside world, both in their conduct and their way of handling matters.

And their level of advancement appeared to far surpass that of other regions under the Five Elders Association.

“If they can rebuild the city, what about those who were taken by the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance?”

“Could they also recreate them out of thin air?”

Curiosity stirred within him. After receiving approval from his true body, Li Qing began secretly observing near the newly built city.

He did not have to wait long.

Six days later, another group of cultivators in radiant battle armor appeared.

They were escorting a massive warship, which hovered above the city.

A gigantic beam of light descended from the ship, enveloping the city below.

Bodies, apparently slumbering—slowly drifted down along the light beam, landing in various parts of the city.

Before long, the once-empty city was filled once more with people.

After the warship completed its task and departed, the sleeping inhabitants gradually awakened.

Yet they seemed completely unaware of what had occurred earlier. It was as if they had been revived from a frozen state, immediately resuming whatever they had been doing before.

In an instant, the city was once again bustling with life and sound.

Li Qing infiltrated the city in secret, controlling one of the residents to examine the situation carefully.

He discovered that these people were indeed real living beings, not mere illusions or constructs.

“This method...”

Li Qing’s expression turned grave.

This war—the Five Elders Association seemed to have lost it.

Yet, in a sense, it felt like they had not truly lost at all.

With newfound anticipation for what might follow, Li Qing turned into a streak of sword light and departed.

...

Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, Guangyang Prefecture.

The Holy Embryo Li Fan was playing Go under a willow tree with a disheveled old man before him.

Li Fan appeared perfectly at ease, while the old man had to ponder for a long time before making each move.

No matter how the old man struggled, he ultimately lost the game.

“Not playing anymore!”

“You brat, there’s something wrong with you!”

The old man snorted, flipping the board in frustration.

Yet strangely enough, the scattered black and white Go pieces did not fall to the ground. Instead, they seemed to be drawn back by the board itself.

They returned neatly to their positions, and the board righted itself, landing once more in front of the old man.

“Damn it all...”

The old man could not help but curse under his breath.

Li Fan, however, was long accustomed to such sights.

This was already the nine hundred and ninety-ninth game between the two of them.
