Chapter 939: Malfunction Of The Law Derivative Jade

“Senior, what are you saying? How am I acting strange?”

“Looking across the entire Xuanhuang Realm, is there anyone else willing to play a thousand rounds of Go with you besides me?”

The Holy Embryo smiled as he spoke, placing a Go piece while asking in return.

That first move landed directly in the Tianyuan position.[1]

The slovenly old man’s expression changed slightly. He looked at the Holy Embryo, his face growing incomparably serious.

“You brat. Clearly you’re only at the Nascent Soul stage, yet your deduction ability is frighteningly strong. And you didn’t even bother hiding it, sitting right down beside me.”

“You obviously came prepared!”

“At first, I was even happy. After all these years, I finally found someone to play with again. But halfway through, I realized—I’d fallen right into your trap!”

The old man sighed lightly.

The Holy Embryo only smiled without replying.

The slovenly old man was named Daoist Qianxun, an old Dao Integration-stage cultivator who had fallen in rank due to injuries.

No one knew what kind of great merit he had once earned for the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, but he actually carried a Derivative Jade Token on him.

Of course, at this point in time, very few people in the world were aware of that.

In the eyes of most, this Daoist Qianxun was nothing more than a somewhat deranged, ordinary cultivator.

It was not until more than ten years later that a mortal named Xiao Shengke happened to encounter Daoist Qianxun.

After spending one thousand days begging alongside him, Xiao was gifted the Derivative Jade Token.

With a mortal body, he was granted audience with Law Derivative Jade, and received the Dao Integration cultivation method, from then on soaring upon the path of immortality.

This story later became a tale widely told among future generations.

The Holy Embryo sought out Daoist Qianxun naturally for that very same Derivative Jade Token.

However, a thousand days of begging was far too long, so he deliberately provoked the old man into playing Go with him.

Daoist Qianxun’s name was closely tied to his cultivation method.[2]

Everything he did required the fulfillment of “a thousand cycles,” and he always needed a companion to accompany him.

Playing Go with the Holy Embryo was, in truth, part of his cultivation.

The only restriction was that he could never take the initiative to explain this principle to others. And every time he completed a phase of “Thousand Searches” cultivation, his appearance and aura would change, becoming like a completely different person.

So everything depended purely on fate.

But Li Fan, the Holy Embryo, already knew this in advance.

When the final piece was placed, Li Fan was short by a single move and ultimately lost to Daoist Qianxun.

“You brat, at least you know to let me win one round.”

Daoist Qianxun cursed angrily. Although he had won, he could not bring himself to feel happy.

He flipped the Go board once more, yet this time the black and white pieces dissolved into thin air.

From his body surged a rippling wave of energy.

“Congratulations, Senior. You’re not far from reintegrating your Dao,” Li Fan said with a smile.

“What do you want?” Daoist Qianxun asked directly, not wasting words.

“The Derivative Jade Token.”

“Get lost!” Daoist Qianxun hurled the jade token over without hesitation.

Before Li Fan could even respond, the old man’s figure vanished.

Li Fan put away the jade token calmly. After all, anyone who had lost nine hundred and ninety-nine games in a row—each time only by a single move—would naturally have a temper.

The fact that Daoist Qianxun did not strike him in anger already showed considerable restraint.

“With the Dao Deviation Stone assisting my divination, there are indeed few in this world who could defeat me at the game of Go,” Li Fan murmured, shaking his head slightly.

He went to a secluded, deserted place and directly activated the Derivative Jade Token.

Beforehand, he had already settled his identity within the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, so the entire process went smoothly without any surprises.

As usual, a cultivator appeared to guide him, and a door materialized from thin air.

However, after a wave of dizziness, Li Fan once again found himself at the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance Headquarters.

“Hm?”

Looking around, it was not the scene of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters.

Within a cluster of hazy light, several figures stood tall.

Faint, intermittent voices came from within.

“On this journey, how certain are you of success?”

“I will do all that I can and leave the rest to fate.”

“That doesn’t sound like something you would say.”

“The difference in strength is too great. All I can do is fight with my life on the line.”

“Enough. Go then. Whether it is fortune or calamity, none can tell.”

…

One of the figures’ voices sounded strangely familiar to Holy Embryo Li Fan, yet it did not exactly match anyone he knew.

He knew this was an illusion caused by entering the Alliance headquarters for the first time, influenced by the Immortal Ancestor.

But this time, for some reason, it was different from all he had seen before, whether with his true body or his clones.

“Among these figures… is one of them the Immortal Ancestor?”

“What does this mean?”

Before Li Fan could think further, the world shook violently, and the scene shattered layer by layer.

He left the illusion and appeared inside the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance headquarters.

The guiding cultivator, long used to such things, gave a brief explanation and led Li Fan toward the location of the Law Derivative Jade.

The journey was smooth, identical to his experiences in past lifetimes.

But when they entered the main hall of the Guardian Pavilion, a notification rang in his ears that left Li Fan completely stunned.

“Applicant for Derivative Jade Token: Fan Li.”

“Number: 005. Permission level: Dao Integration.”

“Please wait patiently. Estimated remaining waiting time: three thousand six hundred twenty-three days and six hours.”

At first, Holy Embryo Li Fan thought he had misheard. But after listening again, he stood up in shock.

“How could it take so long?”

Startled and suspicious, he reacted accordingly and could not help but question the empty air.

“If you do not wish to wait, you may leave.”

“Warning: Leaving will be considered a voluntary forfeiture of your right to use the Derivative Jade Token.”

After a long silence, the voice finally responded.

“I am number 005, yet I must wait nearly ten years. How is that reasonable?”

“Do you think I have never used the Law Derivative Jade before? How long does it take to deduce a single cultivation method? Someone must be abusing their authority!”

Holy Embryo Li Fan showed no fear at all. Instead, he raised a noisy commotion, demanding an explanation.

Perhaps unable to tolerate his shouting, the voice spoke again, colder than before.

“Due to uncontrollable factors, the deduction function of the Law Derivative Jade has suffered severe interference.”

“Therefore, the waiting time has been greatly extended.”

“We ask for your understanding and cooperation.”

“Warning: This matter is a top-level secret of the Immortal Alliance. You must not disclose it.”

The voice issued the warning.

Li Fan’s expression shifted slightly, but he did not restrain himself.

Instead, he exaggeratedly shouted, “What? The very foundation of the Immortal Alliance, the wondrous treasure of the heavens, the Law Derivative Jade—has malfunctioned?”

“What in the world is your Guardian Hall doing?”

“A matter this serious, and instead of fixing it, you choose to cover it up?”

“There must be a traitor among you!”

“No, I will make this public for all to see!”

…

As Holy Embryo Li Fan continued to shout, a figure slowly appeared before him.

Expressionless, the person stared at Li Fan as though looking at a corpse.

Li Fan, however, instantly became serious. “Let me take a look. Perhaps I can help.”

Hearing this, the figure froze in surprise.

[1]: [Tianyuan] – in Go, the "Tianyuan" is the central point of the board (9,9 on a 19x19 grid), a bold and symbolic opening move.

[2]: "Qianxun" means "Thousand Searches" or "Thousand Fathoms," suggesting depth or pursuit.

Chapter 940: The Heart Driven By Greed

The originally vague figure suddenly became clear.

An old man with a full head of white hair, and the character “Fa” engraved on his forehead, immediately appeared before the Holy Embryo Li Fan.

“Do you even know what you are saying?”

“How many Dao Integration cultivators have tried and failed? And yet you, a mere Nascent Soul, dare to speak recklessly?”

The old man’s face darkened as he looked at Li Fan, a hint of murderous intent flashing in his eyes.

Holy Embryo Li Fan was not afraid. He shrugged and said, “What if I do? You don’t really have a better solution anyway, do you?”

This audacious attitude made the old man hesitate slightly.

After a brief investigation into the background of this seemingly arrogant cultivator, he felt something unusual.

This person’s background was unusually clean and simple.

It was as if he had appeared out of nowhere, with crucial information deliberately concealed.

In such cases, either this person was a clone of some great figure, or a spy sent by the Five Elders Association.

“Still, this young one is indeed only at the Nascent Soul stage. No matter what, he cannot turn the tide.”

“And anyway, the situation cannot get any worse than this…”

After a moment’s thought, the white-haired old man snorted coldly. “Follow me.”

As he spoke, Holy Embryo Li Fan felt his vision blur.

When it cleared, he found himself in the same space where Law Derivative Jade had been.

Unlike before, the massive sphere composed of countless constantly shifting characters now seemed corroded. Its rotation and transformations were extremely slow.

Below the sphere, the young girl Law Derivative Jade lay asleep on the long wooden table, her breath heavy and shallow.

Three cultivators stood in the distance, their faces lined with concern as they looked at Law Derivative Jade.

Upon noticing that the white-haired old man had brought Li Fan, they only glanced briefly, asking no further questions.

They quickly returned their attention to the character sphere, which was close to stopping its rotation.

It seemed entangled with some strange force. When Li Fan’s divine sense approached it, the surrounding scene felt as if it had been accelerated a thousandfold. Ř𝐀ƝòᛒË𝙨

Only after he decisively withdrew his divine sense did he realize that in reality, nearly the time of an incense stick had passed in that brief instant.

Li Fan understood that it was not the flow of time around him that had accelerated, but that his own thinking had been slowed to an extreme, almost coming to a standstill.

“How could it become like this?” Holy Embryo Li Fan frowned slightly and transmitted a question to the white-haired old man: “Could it be that this place was invaded by a thief, causing this…”

This was a serious accusation. The white-haired old man’s expression changed slightly, and he interrupted Li Fan: “Do not speak nonsense.”

“All cultivators who had contact with Law Derivative Jade during this period have already been investigated. All suspects have been ruled out. In truth, Law Derivative Jade’s anomaly appeared mysteriously on the very day our Immortal Alliance went to war with the Five Elders Association.”

Li Fan, upon hearing this, instantly envisioned in his mind the five Dharma manifestations, pulling at the massive white net and twisting the principles of heaven and earth within the territory of the Five Elders Association.

"Law Derivative Jade is a Heavenly Wonder of the Xuanhuang Realm, representing a principle of heaven and earth itself."

"Could it be that it was accidentally harmed during this process?"

Li Fan could immediately think of this possibility, and naturally, the Guardians’ Hall would not have failed to consider it.

"The principle of Law Derivative Jade itself has not been affected. We have confirmed this repeatedly," the white-haired elder transmitted calmly to Li Fan, noticing his pensive expression.

"So it is not because of that…" Li Fan nodded and refocused his attention on the character sphere.

Li Fan hesitated for a moment, then sent out another thread of his divine consciousness toward it.

Under normal circumstances, direct contact with Law Derivative Jade’s body was absolutely forbidden.

However, at this moment, with Law Derivative Jade malfunctioning, this attempt at "treatment" went unchallenged.

As his divine consciousness approached the enormous light sphere, the surrounding scenes once again froze.

Ignoring everything else, he continued flying toward the sphere.

Within the perception of his divine sense, space gradually became distorted and twisted.

The sphere covered with characters also unfolded, turning into a flat plane.

It was like a sheet of white paper stretching from heaven to earth, with countless characters flashing and shifting across its surface.

From the perspective of his divine sense, the speed at which Law Derivative Jade unfolded the cultivation techniques seemed normal.

However, this was only because the speed of his own consciousness had been slowed, resulting in both sides being at the same level.

"Could it be caused by the distortion itself?"

His divine sense quickly scanned across the surface of the sheet, roughly outlining the current condition of Law Derivative Jade.

"[Record of Mending Heaven] can heal the ailments of the world."

"Law Derivative Jade is the embodiment of the world’s laws. Once the corresponding symptom is identified…"

"First, we must determine whether the cause of such anomalies is external or from Law Derivative Jade itself."

The thread of divine sense carefully examined everything once again.

For the cultivation world’s "ailments," the Mending Heaven Record has its own system of "observation, listening, inquiry, and pulse-taking."

Just as humans exhibit symptoms when sick, the world itself shows various abnormal signs when afflicted.

Under the scan of his divine consciousness, he indeed found a few clues.

"Unchecked greed, self-delusion arising from the heart…"

His divine consciousness understood, and after repeatedly confirming the diagnosis, it dissipated on its own.

Outside the light sphere, in the vast dark space,

Holy Embryo Li Fan had remained standing motionless for seven days and nights.

The white-haired elder knew he was observing the interior of the Law Derivative Jade light sphere, so he did not intervene.

Instead, he discussed possible solutions with the other three cultivators.

"At most, we can delay for another month."

"If it really does not work, we must quickly summon the [Heavenly Doctor]."

"It’s easy to say. If I could summon him, I would have gone already. Heavenly Doctor’s whereabouts are always elusive, and contacting him is extremely difficult. Moreover, he acts entirely according to his own preference, even ignoring the face of the Immortal Ancestor."

…

The three discussed, brows tightly furrowed.

Meanwhile, Holy Embryo Li Fan gradually regained awareness.

Receiving the information transmitted by his divine consciousness, he nodded silently. Then, almost imperceptibly, his gaze fell on the seemingly sleeping Law Derivative Jade little girl.

"Any progress?" The white-haired elder asked casually, not holding much hope.

To his surprise, Holy Embryo Li Fan nodded: "Almost certain, but it still needs final confirmation."

"You… what did you just say? Almost certain?"

The elder’s astonished reaction immediately drew the attention of the other three Guardians’ Hall cultivators.

In the blink of an eye, they had surrounded the Holy Embryo.

"Kid, it is fine if you lack ability. But do not speak recklessly…”

Chapter 941: Casually Resolving Their Worries

These four were all Dao Integration cultivators, their gazes burning as they stared at the Holy Embryo Li Fan, their own auras surging.

Li Fan showed no trace of nervousness, merely cupping his hands toward the left and right: "Fellow seniors, please stay calm for a moment while I make one final confirmation."

"However, I need to split off a wisp of divine sense to go there!"

With that, Li Fan pointed his index finger at the Law Derivative little girl who was sprawled on the long table, fast asleep.

The four Dao Integration cultivators exchanged glances with one another.

All of them looked troubled.

Li Fan simply waited quietly for them to decide.

"Kid, is there no other way?"

Li Fan replied indifferently: "The saying goes, prescribe the right medicine for the illness. If you misdiagnose the cause and force a treatment, it could instead cause even greater damage."

"It's just the most minute wisp of divine sense, meant only for observation and communication. It probably couldn't even harm an ordinary mortal..."

"If you're truly uneasy about it, then forget it."

The four Dao Integration cultivators discussed it among themselves for a while longer.

In the end, they still decided to let the Holy Embryo Li Fan give it a try.

After Li Fan split off a wisp of divine sense, he immediately felt four different strands of divine sense latch onto it like chains.

The five formed a connected whole.

Everything seen and heard could be fully shared, and they could even communicate with one another.

"Let's go."

Four voices rang out in Li Fan's mind at once.

Knowing this was a necessary precaution, Li Fan had no objections.

In the next moment, the five wisps of divine sense flew together into the Law Derivative little girl's sea of consciousness.

It was a world brimming with radiant golden light, where one cultivation method after another floated about.

Like an ocean of books, vast and boundless.

Upon entering, one's divine sense nearly lost all sense of direction.

Li Fan could clearly see the names of the cultivation methods drifting past him, and if he could, he would even be able to browse their contents freely.

If not for the four powerful divine senses watching him like hawks.

Suppressing the urge to peek at the countless cultivation methods, Li Fan's divine sense manifested a form and shouted loudly toward the surroundings: "Little one, stop pretending to sleep! Come out!"

Though the voice was not loud, in this world filled entirely with cultivation methods, it stood out completely as something alien.

It spread unyieldingly in all directions.

At that moment, the four Dao Integration cultivators' divine senses also manifested forms one after another.

"What did you say? The Law Derivation Jade is pretending to sleep?"

"How is that possible?"

They had never even considered such an outcome, and their faces were full of disbelief as they stared blankly at Li Fan.

Li Fan ignored them entirely and continued calling out on his own: "Little one, do you think pretending to sleep will reduce your workload? What's meant for you will always be yours! You can't escape it!" ℞åƝÓʙËȘ

"When you wake up, won't you still have to keep deriving? You'll even have to make up for what you've owed during this time..."

Li Fan sounded just like a black-hearted boss urging his subordinate to work, chattering on endlessly.

Even the four Guardian Hall Dao Integration cultivators couldn't help but frown slightly upon hearing it.

In this way, who knew how much time passed.

"So noisy!"

A petite figure suddenly appeared above Li Fan's head.

She held a wooden club several times larger than herself and swung it down toward Li Fan's head.

"Zhou..."

"You're awake!"

The four Dao Integration cultivators' faces lit up with joy at the sight.

Though Li Fan's actual combat strength was quite weak, he was not so incapable that he couldn't dodge such an obvious attack.

His form flickered, evading the Law Derivative little girl's strike.

With a swing that missed its mark, the little girl slung the wooden staff over her shoulder and stormed up to Li Fan in a huff: "What do you mean, little brat? This auntie is thousands of years old! I'm not sure how many times older than you!"

Li Fan's expression darkened as he scolded: "If that's the case, then why maintain the appearance of a child and insist on pretending to sleep?"

"Who knows how many people are worried about you!"

The Law Derivative little girl's body trembled, and her momentum weakened instantly.

But soon after, she let out a cold snort and stubbornly insisted: "None of your business! You're so annoying! If it weren't for you, they never would have noticed!"

As she spoke, she quietly touched the massive wooden staff on her shoulder.

She sized up Li Fan, a malicious glint flashing in her eyes.

Li Fan, meanwhile, turned to look at the four Dao Integration experts and cupped his hands in respect.

Everything was understood without words.

"Oh, my little ancestor, why are you throwing a tantrum again..."

The white-haired elder approached the Law Derivative little girl with a wry smile, his face full of helplessness.

"Do you know that in just these few days, the Guardian Hall has nearly ground to a halt? So many urgent matters have been forcibly delayed..."

"What does that have to do with me!" The wooden staff on her shoulder vanished, and the little girl covered her ears, acting as if she heard nothing, nothing at all.

"I've already worked continuously for two thousand three hundred twenty-seven days! That's already exhausting enough! And the vacation I was promised before suddenly disappeared!" she said, puffing out her cheeks in anger.

"I don't care! I want a vacation!"

Who would have thought that this was the reason behind the Derivation Law Jade deliberate feigned sleep.

The four Guardian Hall Dao Integration experts all exchanged helpless, bitter smiles.

"This is just a situation with its reasons..."

The four of them chimed in one after another, constantly comforting and advising her.

It took quite some effort before they finally persuaded the Law Derivative little girl to change her mind.

She agreed to rest just one more day before resuming work.

With that said, the little girl vanished in a huff.

The others were also forcibly ejected from the girl's sea of consciousness.

Returning to their own bodies, the group looked toward the distant orb where the characters' transformations had returned to normal speed, and they all let out a sigh of relief.

"Little brother, may I ask how you saw through the Law Derivative Jade pretense of sleep?"

The white-haired elder turned to Li Fan at this moment, asking curiously.

His attitude had softened considerably, no longer as cold as before.

"It's a unique secret technique; I cannot disclose it." Li Fan showed no flattered surprise at being treated as an equal by Dao Integration cultivators; he simply refused to answer the question.

A trace of embarrassment flashed across the white-haired elder's face.

But it vanished quickly, and he did not flare up.

In his view, this young cultivator had managed to discern the "ailment" of the Law Derivative Jade at a glance.

His attainments in "medicine" were likely extraordinary.

His true self, even if not a Dao Integration cultivator, could not be much weaker.

"Heh, no matter. As long as the Law Derivative Jade has awakened, that's what counts." The other three Dao Integration experts chuckled and smoothed things over.

"Speaking of which, after helping you resolve such a major issue, there should be some reward, right?" Li Fan suddenly asked at this point.

The four were slightly taken aback.

The white-haired elder reacted the fastest: "Of course! There certainly will be!"

"Rewards are easy to discuss. It's just that the matter of the Law Derivative Jade malfunctioning still requires the little brother to help keep it confidential."

"What would the little brother like?"

Li Fan pondered for a moment before replying: "I haven't thought of anything yet. If I have a need in the future, I'll come find you. How about that?"

The white-haired elder stared intently at Li Fan for a moment, then did not refuse.

Instead, he handed a jade pendant to Li Fan as a token of identity.

With everything back on track, Li Fan returned next to the waiting space.

Following the previous queue order, he waited for the derivation of his technique.

Chapter 942: Clever Words Tempting The Little Girl

After the Law Derivative little girl resumed her normal work, the waiting time required was no longer as long as ten years.

Just four days later, the Holy Embryo Li Fan returned once more to the space where the Law Derivative Jade was located.

The little girl, who was focused on her work at the desk, seemed to sense Li Fan's arrival and suddenly raised her head.

Upon seeing that the visitor was indeed Li Fan, her big eyes darted around slyly, and a mischievous thought immediately arose in her mind.

The little girl sat up straight and proper, her face full of seriousness.

When Li Fan approached, she slapped the table with both hands and said in a childish voice, "What kind of technique do you want to derive? What are the requirements for the technique? Is it deriving from nothing or from a blueprint technique?"

"Write it down yourself first."

With that, a sheet of white paper flew toward Li Fan.

She seemed to have completely forgotten the matter of Li Fan treating her illness before and was now solely focused on deriving the technique.

Li Fan caught the paper, and rows of words reflecting his inner thoughts quickly appeared on it.

"Using the 'Three Perils of Ghosts and Spirits in the Netherworld Record' as the blueprint technique, derive a technique that resembles it in form."

"Specific requirements: After cultivating the technique, the aura and characteristics revealed during operation must be identical to those of the 'Three Perils of Ghosts and Spirits in the Netherworld Record.' However, the core must actually be different; it needs to be a completely new technique..."

Dense lines of small characters soon filled the entire page.

But the paper did not grow larger; instead, after the font shrank overall, new blank areas appeared.

This cycle repeated itself, shrinking more than ten times, yet still not filling the paper.

Although this was due to the complexity of the 'Three Perils of Ghosts and Spirits in the Netherworld Record' technique, the unusual scene still alerted the Dao Integration guardian in this space.

Li Fan keenly sensed a strand of divine sense sweeping over him.

Just as he was about to explain, the Law Derivative little girl took the initiative to clear his name: "Don't worry; it's really just a simple technique. Not an [Evil Curse]..."

The little girl propped her chin up with both hands and stared blankly ahead.

Symbols seemed to flicker in sync within her big eyes, like streams of data.

Only then did the divine sense locked on the Holy Embryo Li Fan withdraw.

Li Fan continued writing on the paper until he finally finished.

The white paper turned into a streak of light and flew back into the Law Derivative Jade's hands.

The little girl lowered her head and fell into deep thought, remaining silent for a long time.

"Um..."

"The blueprint technique is a bit complicated. And your requirements are quite numerous."

"Also, there are a few questions that need you to explain in detail..."

After pretending to ponder for a moment, the Law Derivative Jade little girl suddenly slapped the table again: "To prevent your technique from leaking, come with me!"

Before Li Fan could respond, a beam of light shot out from the giant spherical array of characters above, enveloping him.

In the next moment, the Holy Embryo Li Fan vanished from this space.

At the same time, the Law Derivative Jade little girl at the long table also disappeared.

The white-haired Dao Integration cultivator revealed his form, his face full of helplessness.

He knew the child's temperament of the Law Derivative Jade. Having been exposed by this "Fan Li" for faking illness before, now encountering him again, she was certainly going to get revenge and vent her pent-up anger.

However, it was only playful mischief and would not cause any serious consequences.

The white-haired Dao Integration cultivator simply did not stop her.

"Letting her occasionally have a punching bag to vent on isn't bad; it saves her from going on strike at the drop of a hat," the white-haired Dao Integration cultivator thought instead.

In the Endless Book Sea World.

As soon as the Holy Embryo Li Fan entered this place, he felt that from sky to earth, over a hundred wooden sticks surrounded him airtight and smashed toward him all at once.

"Hold on!"

At the critical moment, Li Fan shouted loudly.

The wooden staff halted one inch before the Holy Embryo Li Fan.

The Law Derivative little girl appeared above him. She placed her hands on her hips and looked down at Li Fan with great smugness: "How about it! Do you know you were wrong now?"

"Apologize to me properly, and I will spare you!"

"Otherwise..."

"Hmph hmph!"

As if to demonstrate, one of the wooden staffs lightly tapped Li Fan on the forehead.

Li Fan sized up the Law Derivative little girl with a half-smile.

His gaze made her heart tighten at once: "What are you going to do..."

"I warn you, do not make a move!"

"If we really fight, you are no match for me. I... I have not fought anyone for many years. I am afraid I might accidentally kill you!"

The Law Derivative little girl waved her small fists and threatened him huffily.

Li Fan merely smiled and shook his head, saying nothing.

This only made the Law Derivative little girl even more annoyed: "You are truly detestable!"

"Will you admit your mistake or not? If you do not apologize to me soon, I will really beat you!"

Li Fan ignored the wooden staffs that were almost pressed against his face and sighed: "It seems... your illness has not fully recovered yet."

Upon hearing the Holy Embryo Li Fan's words, the Law Derivative little girl shuddered: "What illness? I have no illness!"

Though the Law Derivative little girl tried her best to conceal it, the panic on her small face was genuine.

Li Fan chuckled: "If you have no illness, why do you keep thinking of slacking off and resting at every turn?"

"On the surface, you seek trouble with me, but in truth..."

"You just want to take this opportunity to rest again! You cannot even spare such a short time; how lazy you are!" Li Fan's gaze was piercing as he said sternly.

The Law Derivative little girl's body trembled: "You... how can you slander me like this!"

She stared at Li Fan with a face full of grievance, as if tears were about to fall.

Li Fan remained expressionless and was not swayed by her.

But after a brief silence, he changed his tone: "However, this is not your fault."

"Tension and relaxation in balance is the natural way. Anyone who works continuously for so long would want to rest."

Unexpectedly, the other party was speaking up for her. The Law Derivative little girl's eyes widened in surprise, and she nodded repeatedly.

"Moreover, in my view, your repeated strikes and laziness are not from your own will. It is merely the instinct of your body driving you."

"As the saying goes, excess indulgence leads to ignorance of one's heart. It is like how mortals drink when thirsty and eat when hungry. It is your innate nature, unavoidable."

The Holy Embryo Li Fan spoke eloquently. The Law Derivative little girl had never heard such a theory before and was deeply drawn in.

The wooden staffs that filled the sky vanished at once. She pondered and said: "So that is how it is. No wonder I could not control my desire to slack off this time, yet every time I did, a sense of guilt always lingered in my heart."

"This is the affliction of [heart ignorance]." Li Fan sighed.

The little girl looked at Li Fan, who had the benevolent expression of a healer, and asked curiously: "Is this also an illness? Can it be cured?"

After all, the other party had seen through her feigned sleep at a glance. The little girl trusted Li Fan's medical skills.

Moreover, no one had ever discussed these things with her, so she could not help but follow Li Fan's train of thought and voice her inner doubts naturally.

Li Fan smiled: "To cure this illness is difficult if said to be difficult, and easy if said to be easy."

"The greed in your heart arises solely from prolonged lack of rest.”

Chapter 943: Seeing the Bones in the Pavilion Again

The voice of the Holy Embryo Li Fan echoed in this endless sea of merit laws.

She could not help but say, "How could anyone possibly find idleness boring?! If I did not have to derive techniques, I would definitely sleep for a few decades first!"

As if yearning for that wonderful vacation life, the little girl's face was full of longing.

However, she soon said in great dejection, "Sigh, forget about resting for a few decades. Even taking a break for ten days or half a month would be extremely difficult."
